SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties

- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
     9	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    10	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    11	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    12	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    13	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    15	
    16	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    17	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    18	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    19	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    20	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    21	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    22	            )
    23	        }
    24	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    25	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    26	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    27	        )
    28	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    29	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    30	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    31	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    32	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        }
    34	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    35	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    36	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    37	        }
    38	        setContent {
    39	            CinerificTheme {
    40	                CinerificApp()
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	    }
    44	}

   123	@Composable
   124	fun CinerificApp() {
   125	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   126	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   127	    var signInSessionId by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
   128	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   129	    var introSnapshot by rememberSaveable(stateSaver = CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver) {
   130	        mutableStateOf(CinerificIntroSnapshot())
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    if (showHome) {
   134	        CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   135	            CinerificMainExperience(
   136	                signInSessionId = signInSessionId,
   137	                signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   138	                selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   139	                onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   140	                onSignOut = {
   141	                    signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   142	                    introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   143	                    showHome = false
   144	                }
   145	            )
   146	        }
   147	    } else {
   148	        AndroidView(
   149	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   150	            factory = { context ->
   151	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   152	                    onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   153	                        if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   154	                            introSnapshot = snapshot
   155	                        }
   156	                    }
   157	                    restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   158	                    onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   159	                        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   160	                        signedInProfile = profile
   161	                        signInSessionId += 1
   162	                        showHome = true
   163	                    }
   164	                }
   165	            },
   166	            update = { view ->
   167	                view.onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   168	                    if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   169	                        introSnapshot = snapshot
   170	                    }
   171	                }
   172	                view.restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   173	                view.onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   174	                    introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   175	                    signedInProfile = profile
   176	                    signInSessionId += 1
   177	                    showHome = true
   178	                }
   179	            }
   180	        )
   181	    }
   182	}
   183	
   184	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   185	    Home,
   186	    Movies,
   187	    Shows,
   188	    Favorites,
   189	    Settings,
   190	    ProgramDetails
   191	}
   192	
   193	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   194	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   195	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   196	) {
   197	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   198	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   199	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   200	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)
   201	}
   202	
   203	@Composable
   204	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   205	    signInSessionId: Int,
   206	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   207	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   208	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   209	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   210	) {
   211	    var destination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   212	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   213	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   214	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   215	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   216	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   217	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   218	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   219	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   220	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   221	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   222	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   223	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   224	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   225	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   226	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   227	            },
   228	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   229	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   230	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   231	            }
   232	        )
   233	    }
   234	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   235	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   236	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   237	        catalogRouteModeName
   238	    ) {
   239	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   240	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   241	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   242	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   243	        )
   244	    }
   245	
   246	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   247	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   248	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   249	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   250	    }
   251	
   252	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   253	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   254	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   255	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   256	        if (
   257	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   258	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   259	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   260	        ) {
   261	            onSignOut()
   262	        }
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   266	        clearCatalogRoute()
   267	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   268	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   269	    }
   270	
   271	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   272	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   273	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   274	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   275	        destination = route.destination
   276	    }
   277	
   278	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   279	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   280	    }
   281	
   282	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   283	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   284	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   285	            return
   286	        }
   287	
   288	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   289	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   290	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   291	            isShow = isShow
   292	        )
   293	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   294	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   295	            return
   296	        }
   297	
   298	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   299	    }
   300	
   301	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   302	        Box(
   303	            modifier = Modifier
   304	                .fillMaxSize()
   305	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   306	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   307	                        while (true) {
   308	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   309	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   310	                        }
   311	                    }
   312	                }
   313	        ) {
   314	            when (destination) {
   315	                CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   316	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   317	                    onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   318	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   319	                )
   320	                CinerificDestination.Movies,
   321	                CinerificDestination.Shows,
   322	                CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   323	                CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   324	                    destination = destination,
   325	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   326	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   327	                    onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   328	                    autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   329	                    onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   330	                        autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   331	                        lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   332	                    },
   333	                    onSignOut = onSignOut,
   334	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   335	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   336	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   337	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   338	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   339	                    catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   340	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   341	                )
   342	                CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   343	                    programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   344	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   345	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   346	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   347	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   348	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   349	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   350	                )
   351	            }
   352	
   353	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   354	                currentDestination = destination,
   355	                onDestinationSelected = {
   356	                    clearCatalogRoute()
   357	                    destination = it
   358	                },
   359	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   360	            )
   361	
   362	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   363	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   364	                currentDestination = destination,
   365	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   366	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   367	            )
   368	        }
   369	    }
   370	}
   371	
   372	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   373	    destinationName: String,
   374	    genreName: String,
   375	    modeName: String
   376	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   377	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   378	
   379	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   380	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   381	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   382	
   383	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   384	        destination = destination,
   385	        genre = genre,
   386	        mode = mode
   387	    )
   388	}
   389	
   390	@Composable
   391	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   392	    requestId: Int,
   393	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   394	    suppressed: Boolean,
   395	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   396	) {
   397	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   398	
   399	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   400	        if (suppressed) {
   401	            visible = false
   402	            return@LaunchedEffect
   403	        }
   404	        if (requestId == 0) {
   405	            visible = false
   406	            return@LaunchedEffect
   407	        }
   408	        visible = true
   409	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   410	        visible = false
   411	    }
   412	
   413	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   414	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   415	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   416	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   417	    )
   418	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   419	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   420	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   421	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   422	    )
   423	
   424	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   425	
   426	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   427	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   428	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   429	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   430	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   431	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   432	        } else {
   433	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   434	        }
   435	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   436	            stageScale = stageScale,
   437	            modifier = Modifier
   438	                .absoluteOffset(
   439	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   440	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
   441	                )
   442	                .graphicsLayer {
   443	                    this.alpha = alpha
   444	                    scaleX = promptScale
   445	                    scaleY = promptScale
   446	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   447	                }
   448	        )
   449	    }
   450	}
   451	
   452	@Composable
   453	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   454	    stageScale: Float,
   455	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   456	) {
   457	    Box(
   458	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   459	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   460	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   461	        )
   462	    ) {
   463	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   464	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   465	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   466	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   467	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   468	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   469	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   470	                close()
   471	            }
   472	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   473	            drawRoundRect(
   474	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   475	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   476	                size = Size(
   477	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
   478	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
   479	                ),
   480	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   481	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   482	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   483	                )
   484	            )
   485	            drawRoundRect(
   486	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   487	                topLeft = Offset(
   488	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
   489	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
   490	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
   491	                ),
   492	                size = Size(
   493	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
   494	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
   495	                ),
   496	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   497	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   498	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   499	                ),
   500	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
   501	            )
   502	        }
   503	        Text(
   504	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
   505	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   506	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   507	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
   508	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   509	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
   510	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   511	            modifier = Modifier
   512	                .absoluteOffset(
   513	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
   514	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
   515	                )
   516	                .requiredSize(
   517	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   518	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   519	                )
   520	        )
   521	    }
   522	}
   523	
   524	@Composable
   525	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
   526	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   527	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
   528	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   529	        p,
   530	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   531	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   532	    )
   533	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
   534	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
   535	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
   536	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
   537	    )
   538	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   539	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   540	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   541	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   542	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
   543	
   544	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   545	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   546	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
   547	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
   548	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
   549	
   550	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
   551	
   552	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   553	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   554	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   555	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   556	                end = baseBounds,
   557	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   558	            )
   559	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   560	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   561	                end = baseBounds,
   562	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   563	            )
   564	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   565	
   566	            drawFigmaImage(
   567	                image = images.logoSimple,
   568	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   569	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   570	                stageTop = stageTop,
   571	                stageScale = stageScale,
   572	                alpha = logoAlpha
   573	            )
   574	            drawFigmaImage(
   575	                image = images.logoEyes,
   576	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   577	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   578	                stageTop = stageTop,
   579	                stageScale = stageScale,
   580	                alpha = logoAlpha
   581	            )
   582	        }
   583	
   584	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
   585	            val y = avatarYOffset
   586	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   587	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   588	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   589	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   590	
   591	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   592	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   593	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   594	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   595	        }
   596	    }
   597	}

   605	@Composable
   606	private fun IntroFrame2FromFigma() {
   607	    // Node 474:7311 with Logo Slide 1 instance 478:6917.
   608	    val slideYOffset = remember { Animatable(-417f) }
   609	
   610	    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
   611	        slideYOffset.snapTo(-417f)
   612	        slideYOffset.animateTo(
   613	            targetValue = 0f,
   614	            animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 1800, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing)
   615	        )
   616	    }
   617	
   618	    FigmaStage(background = solidBrush(ColorFrame2Background)) { scale ->
   619	        LogoSlide1Layer(scale = scale, yOffset = slideYOffset.value)
   620	    }
   621	}

   642	    // Node 478:6975 + Star Overlay 482:7696.

   645	            scale = scale,
   646	            darkOverlay = 0.5f,

   648	        )

   651	            logoResId = R.drawable.logo_simple_signin,
   652	            eyesResId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_signin
   653	        )
   654

   656	        Box(
   657	            modifier = Modifier
   658	                .absoluteOffset(x = figma(894f, scale), y = figma(266f, scale))
   659	                .requiredSize(figma(200f, scale), figma(200f, scale))
   660	                .clip(CircleShape)
   661	                .background(
   662	                    Brush.radialGradient(
   663	                        colors = listOf(Color(0xFFE8E8E8), Color(0xFF8A8A8A), Color(0xFF1D1D1D))
   664	                    )
   665	                )
   666	        )
   667	
   668	        // Node 485:7113 loading spinner instance, rebuilt from centered standalone layers.
   669	        val spinnerHeight = 300f *
   670	            CINERIFIC_LOADING_SPINNER_CANVAS_HEIGHT /
   671	            CINERIFIC_LOADING_SPINNER_CANVAS_WIDTH
   672	        CinerificLoadingSpinner(
   673	            modifier = Modifier
   674	                .absoluteOffset(
   675	                    x = figma(447f, scale),
   676	                    y = figma(267f - (spinnerHeight - 300f) / 2f, scale)
   677	                )
   678	                .requiredSize(figma(300f, scale), figma(spinnerHeight, scale))
   679	        )
   680	    }
   681	}

   761	    val logoEyes: ImageBitmap,

   764	    val jannyAvatar: ImageBitmap,

   769	    val guestName: ImageBitmap
   770	)

   772	@Composable
   773	private fun rememberIntroImages(): IntroImages {
   774	    val resources = LocalContext.current.resources
   775	    return remember(resources) {
   776	        IntroImages(
   777	            logoSimple = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.logo_simple_large).asImageBitmap(),
   778	            logoEyes = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.logo_eyes_large).asImageBitmap(),
   779	            steveAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   780	            martinAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   781	            jannyAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   782	            guestAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   783	            steveName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.steve_name).asImageBitmap(),
   784	            martinName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.martin_name).asImageBitmap(),
   785	            jannyName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.janny_name).asImageBitmap(),
   786	            guestName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.guest_name).asImageBitmap()
   787	        )
   788	    }

   790	
   791	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
   792	    image: ImageBitmap,
   793	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
   794	    stageLeft: Float,
   795	    stageTop: Float,
   796	    stageScale: Float,
   797	    alpha: Float = 1f,
   798	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
   799	) {
   800	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
   801	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
   802	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
   803	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
   804	
   805	    if (clipCircle) {
   806	        val clip = Path().apply {
   807	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
   808	        }
   809	        clipPath(clip) {
   810	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   811	        }
   812	    } else {
   813	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   814	    }
   815	}
   816	
   817	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
   818	    image: ImageBitmap,
   819	    left: Float,
   820	    top: Float,
   821	    width: Float,
   822	    height: Float,
   823	    alpha: Float
   824	) {
   825	    drawImage(
   826	        image = image,
   827	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
   828	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
   829	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   830	    )
   831	}

   833	@Composable
   834	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
   835	    scale: Float,
   836	    entryProgress: Float,
   837	    finalProgress: Float,
   838	    alpha: Float
   839	) {
   840	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   841	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   842	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   843	        end = baseBounds,
   844	        amount = entryProgress
   845	    )
   846	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   847	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   848	        end = baseBounds,
   849	        amount = entryProgress
   850	    )
   851	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   852	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   853	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   854	
   855	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   856	        base = baseBounds,
   857	        target = simpleBounds,
   858	        scale = scale,
   859	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
   860	        alpha = alpha
   861	    )
   862	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   863	        base = baseBounds,
   864	        target = eyesBounds,
   865	        scale = scale,
   866	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
   867	        alpha = alpha
   868	    )
   869	}

   871	@Composable
   872	private fun AvatarSelectionLayer(scale: Float, alpha: Float, yOffset: Float) {
   873	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
   874	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   875	    val isLandscape = configuration.screenWidthDp > configuration.screenHeightDp
   876	    val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(isLandscape)
   877	    val accountPromptExtraShiftY = signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape)
   878	    val accountPromptTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape)
   879	    val accountPromptSecondaryTextSize = accountPromptTextSize * ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SECONDARY_TEXT_SCALE
   880	    Box(
   881	        modifier = Modifier
   882	            .requiredSize(figma(1194f, scale), figma(834f, scale))
   883	            .graphicsLayer {
   884	                this.alpha = alpha
   885	                translationY = yOffset * scale * density
   886	            }
   887	    ) {
   888	        // Figma Logo Slide 2, Property 1=Variant2, constrained inside Sign-in Frame.
   889	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 152f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   890	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 390f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   891	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 628f, y = 454f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   892	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 866f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   893	
   894	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 130f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_name)
   895	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 368f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_name)
   896	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 606f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_name)
   897	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 854f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 200f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_name)
   898	        SignInPromptText(
   899	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
   900	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LEFT_ANCHOR_X,
   901	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   902	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   903	            textAlign = TextAlign.Left,
   904	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   905	            scale = scale
   906	        )
   907	        SignInPromptText(
   908	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
   909	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH / 2f,
   910	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   911	            textSize = accountPromptTextSize,
   912	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   913	            scale = scale
   914	        )
   915	        SignInPromptText(
   916	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
   917	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_RIGHT_ANCHOR_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH,
   918	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   919	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   920	            textAlign = TextAlign.Right,
   921	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   922	            scale = scale
   923	        )
   924	    }
   925	}
   926	
   927	@Composable
   928	private fun SignInPromptText(
   929	    text: String,
   930	    x: Float,
   931	    y: Float,
   932	    textSize: Float,
   933	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
   934	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   935	    scale: Float
   936	) {
   937	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
   938	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
   939	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
   940	        else -> Alignment.Center
   941	    }
   942	    Box(
   943	        modifier = Modifier
   944	            .absoluteOffset(
   945	                x = figma(x, scale),
   946	                y = figma(y, scale)
   947	            )
   948	            .requiredSize(
   949	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
   950	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
   951	            ),
   952	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
   953	    ) {
   954	        Text(
   955	            text = text,
   956	            color = Color.White,
   957	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   958	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
   959	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
   960	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
   961	            maxLines = 1,
   962	            softWrap = false,
   963	            textAlign = textAlign
   964	        )
   965	    }
   966	}
   967	
   968	@Composable
   969	private fun FigmaAvatarImage(
   970	    x: Float,
   971	    y: Float,
   972	    w: Float,
   973	    h: Float,
   974	    scale: Float,
   975	    @DrawableRes resId: Int
   976	) {
   977	    Image(
   978	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
   979	        contentDescription = null,
   980	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
   981	        modifier = Modifier
   982	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   983	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
   984	            .clip(CircleShape)
   985	    )
   986	}
   987	
   988	@Composable
   989	private fun LogoDot(x: Float, y: Float, scale: Float, active: Boolean) {
   990	    Box(
   991	        modifier = Modifier
   992	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   993	            .requiredSize(figma(50.4f, scale), figma(50.4f, scale))
   994	            .clip(CircleShape)
   995	            .background(if (active) Color(0xFFE7E7E7) else Color(0xFF050505))
   996	    )
   997	}
   998	
   999	@Composable
  1000	private fun PromoBackgroundLayer(
  1001	    scale: Float,
  1002	    darkOverlay: Float,
  1003	    @DrawableRes backgroundResId: Int,
  1004	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1005	) {
  1006	    // Nodes 793:7049 / 793:7050 at x:0 y:0 size:1584x1584.
  1007	    FigmaAssetImage(
  1008	        x = 0f,
  1009	        y = 0f,
  1010	        w = 1584f,
  1011	        h = 1584f,
  1012	        scale = scale,
  1013	        resId = backgroundResId,
  1014	        alpha = alpha
  1015	    )
  1016	
  1017	    if (darkOverlay > 0f) {
  1018	        Box(
  1019	            modifier = Modifier
  1020	                .absoluteOffset(x = figma(0f, scale), y = figma(0f, scale))
  1021	                .requiredSize(figma(1584f, scale), figma(1584f, scale))
  1022	                .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1023	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = darkOverlay))
  1024	        )
  1025	    }
  1026	}
  1027	
  1028	@Composable
  1029	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1030	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1031	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1032	    scale: Float,
  1033	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1034	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1035	) {
  1036	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1037	    Image(
  1038	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1039	        contentDescription = null,
  1040	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1041	        modifier = Modifier
  1042	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1043	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1044	            .graphicsLayer {
  1045	                this.alpha = alpha
  1046	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1047	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1048	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1049	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1050	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1051	            }
  1052	    )
  1053	}
  1054	
  1055	@Composable
  1056	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1057	    x: Float,
  1058	    y: Float,
  1059	    w: Float,
  1060	    h: Float,
  1061	    scale: Float,
  1062	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1063	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1064	) {
  1065	    Image(
  1066	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1067	        contentDescription = null,
  1068	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1069	        modifier = Modifier
  1070	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1071	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1072	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1073	    )
  1074	}
  1075	
  1076	@Composable
  1077	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1078	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1079	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1080	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1081	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1082	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1083	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1084	
  1085	        Box(
  1086	            modifier = Modifier
  1087	                .fillMaxSize()
  1088	                .background(background)
  1089	        ) {
  1090	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1091	                modifier = Modifier
  1092	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1093	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1094	            ) {
  1095	                content(scale)
  1096	            }
  1097	        }
  1098	    }
  1099	}
  1100	
  1101	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1102	
  1103	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1104	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1105	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1106	    } else {
  1107	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1108	    }
  1109	}
  1110	
  1111	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1112	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1113	}
  1114	
  1115	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1116	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1117	}
  1118	
  1119	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1120	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1121	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1122	        colors = listOf(
  1123	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1124	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1125	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1126	        )
  1127	    )
  1128	}
  1129	
  1130	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1131	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1132	)
  1133	
  1134	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1135	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1136	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1137	}
  1138	
  1139	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1140	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1141	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1142	}
  1143	
  1144	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1145	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1146	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1147	}
  1148	
  1149	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1150	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1151	}
  1152	
  1153	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1154	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1155	}
  1156	
  1157	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1158	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1159	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1160	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1161	    return base + bounce
  1162	}
  1163	
  1164	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1165	
  1166	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1167	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1168	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1169	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1170	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1171	)
  1172	
  1173	private val CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver = listSaver<CinerificIntroSnapshot, Any>(
  1174	    save = { snapshot ->
  1175	        listOf(
  1176	            snapshot.activeFlowName,
  1177	            snapshot.usernameText,
  1178	            snapshot.passwordText,
  1179	            snapshot.confirmPasswordText,
  1180	            snapshot.emailText,
  1181	            snapshot.monthText,
  1182	            snapshot.dayText,
  1183	            snapshot.yearText,
  1184	            snapshot.subscriptionTierText,
  1185	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName,
  1186	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName,
  1187	            snapshot.rememberMeChecked,
  1188	            snapshot.createAvatarIndex,
  1189	            snapshot.portraitProfileIndex
  1190	        )
  1191	    },
  1192	    restore = { values ->
  1193	        CinerificIntroSnapshot(
  1194	            activeFlowName = values.getOrNull(0) as? String ?: "",
  1195	            usernameText = values.getOrNull(1) as? String ?: "",
  1196	            passwordText = values.getOrNull(2) as? String ?: "",
  1197	            confirmPasswordText = values.getOrNull(3) as? String ?: "",
  1198	            emailText = values.getOrNull(4) as? String ?: "",
  1199	            monthText = values.getOrNull(5) as? String ?: "Mon",
  1200	            dayText = values.getOrNull(6) as? String ?: "Day",
  1201	            yearText = values.getOrNull(7) as? String ?: "Year",
  1202	            subscriptionTierText = values.getOrNull(8) as? String ?: "Subscription Tier",
  1203	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = values.getOrNull(9) as? String ?: "Password",
  1204	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = values.getOrNull(10) as? String ?: "Idle",
  1205	            rememberMeChecked = values.getOrNull(11) as? Boolean ?: false,
  1206	            createAvatarIndex = values.getOrNull(12) as? Int ?: 0,
  1207	            portraitProfileIndex = values.getOrNull(13) as? Int ?: 0
  1208	        )
  1209	    }
  1210	)
