   438	
   439	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   440	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   441	
   442	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   443	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   444	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   445	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   446	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   447	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   448	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   449	
   450	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   451	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   452	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   453	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   454	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   455	        pressedMockField = null
   456	        pressedRememberMe = false
   457	        pressedBackChevron = false
   458	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   459	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   460	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   461	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   462	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   463	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   464	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   465	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   466	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   467	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   468	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   469	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   470	        activeComposingText = ""
   471	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   472	        focusedMockField = null
   473	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   474	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   475	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   476	
   477	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   478	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   479	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   480	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   481	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   482	        } else {
   483	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   484	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   485	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   486	        }
   487	
   488	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   489	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   490	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   491	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   492	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   493	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   494	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   495	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   496	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   497	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   498	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   499	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   500	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   501	        ) {
   502	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   503	        } else {
   504	            null
   505	        }
   506	
   507	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   508	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   509	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   510	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   511	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   512	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   513	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   514	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   515	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   516	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   517	        }
   518	
   519	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   520	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   521	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   522	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   523	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   524	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   525	    }
   526	
   527	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   528	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   529	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   530	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   531	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   532	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   533	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   534	        pressedMockField = null
   535	        pressedRememberMe = false
   536	        pressedBackChevron = false
   537	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   538	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   539	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   540	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   541	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   542	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   543	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   544	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   545	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   546	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   547	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   548	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   549	    }
   550	
   551	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   552	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   553	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   554	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   555	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   556	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   557	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   558	    }
   559	
   560	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   561	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   562	
   563	        if (IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis == 0L) {
   564	            IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   565	        }
   566	        val progress = bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis)
   567	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   568	            progress,
   569	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   570	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   571	        )
   572	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   573	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   574	
   575	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   576	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   577	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   578	        finishPortraitProfileCarouselIfNeeded()
   579	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   580	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   581	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   582	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   583	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   584	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   585	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   586	
   587	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   588	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   589	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha, formFocusMotion)
   590	        }
   591	
   592	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   593	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   594	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   595	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   596	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   597	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   598	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   599	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
   600	                    drawPortraitProfile(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   601	                } else {
   602	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   603	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   604	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   605	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   606	
   607	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   608	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   609	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   610	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   611	                }
   612	            }
   613	        }
   614	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   615	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   616	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   617	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   618	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   619	                    canvas,
   620	                    outgoingFlow,
   621	                    stage.left,
   622	                    stage.top,
   623	                    stage.scale,
   624	                    1f - switchMotion,
   625	                    formFocusMotion,
   626	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   627	                )
   628	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   629	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   630	                        canvas,
   631	                        incomingFlow,
   632	                        stage.left,
   633	                        stage.top,
   634	                        stage.scale,
   635	                        switchMotion,
   636	                        formFocusMotion,
   637	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   638	                    )
   639	                }
   640	            } else {
   641	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   642	            }
   643	            drawBackChevron(
   644	                canvas,
   645	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   646	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   647	                stage.left,
   648	                accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   649	                stage.scale,
   650	                mockMotion
   651	            )
   652	        }
   653	        drawAccountPrompt(
   654	            canvas = canvas,
   655	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   656	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   657	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   658	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   659	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   660	            } else {
   661	                0f
   662	            },
   663	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   664	        )
   665	
   666	        if (
   667	            progress < 1f ||
   668	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   669	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   670	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   671	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   672	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   673	            isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating() ||
   674	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   675	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   676	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   677	        ) {
   678	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   679	        }
   680	    }
   681	
   682	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   683	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   684	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   685	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   686	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   687	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   688	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
   689	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   690	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   691	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   692	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   693	                        return true
   694	                    }
   695	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   696	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   697	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   698	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   699	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   700	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   701	                    if (
   702	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   703	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   704	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   705	                    ) {
   706	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   707	                        return true
   708	                    }
   709	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   710	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   711	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   712	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   713	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   714	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   715	                    } else {
   716	                        null
   717	                    }
   718	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   719	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   720	                    } else {
   721	                        null
   722	                    }
   723	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   724	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   725	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   726	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   727	                    ) {
   728	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   729	                    } else {
   730	                        null
   731	                    }
   732	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   733	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   734	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   735	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   736	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   737	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   738	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   739	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   740	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   741	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   742	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   743	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   744	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   745	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   746	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   747	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   748	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   749	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   750	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   751	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   752	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   753	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   754	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   755	                    ) {
   756	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   757	                    } else {
   758	                        null
   759	                    }
   760	                    pressedMockField = if (
   761	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   762	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   763	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   764	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   765	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   766	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   767	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   768	                    ) {
   769	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   770	                    } else {
   771	                        null
   772	                    }
   773	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   774	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   775	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   776	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   777	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   778	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   779	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   780	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   781	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   782	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   783	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   784	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   785	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   786	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   787	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   788	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   789	                    return true
   790	                }
   791	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   792	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   793	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   794	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   795	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   796	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   797	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   798	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   799	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   800	                    isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()
   801	                ) {
   802	                    null
   803	                } else {
   804	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   805	                }
   806	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   807	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   808	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   809	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   810	            }
   811	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   812	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   813	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   814	                        return true
   815	                    }
   816	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   817	                        return true
   818	                    }
   819	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   820	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   821	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   822	                    }
   823	                    return true
   824	                }
   825	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   826	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   827	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   828	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   829	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   830	                    }
   831	                    if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved) {
   832	                        portraitProfileDragDeltaX = horizontalDelta
   833	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   834	                    }
   835	                    return true
   836	                }
   837	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   838	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   839	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   840	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   841	            }
   842	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   843	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   844	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   845	                    pressedMockField = null
   846	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   847	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   848	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   849	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   850	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   851	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   852	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   853	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   854	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   855	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   856	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   857	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   858	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   859	                    return true
   860	                }
   861	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   862	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   863	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   864	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   865	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   866	                        return true
   867	                    }
   868	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   869	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   870	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   871	                        }
   872	                        pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   873	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   874	                        }
   875	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt && settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   876	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   877	                        }
   878	                        else -> null
   879	                    }
   880	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   881	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   882	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   883	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   884	                        super.performClick()
   885	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   886	                        return true
   887	                    }
   888	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   889	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   890	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   891	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   892	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   893	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   894	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   895	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   896	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   897	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   898	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   899	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   900	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   901	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   902	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   903	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   904	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   905	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   906	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   907	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   908	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   909	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   910	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   911	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   912	                        pressedOption != null &&
   913	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   914	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   915	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   916	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   917	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   918	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   919	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   920	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   921	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   922	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   923	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   924	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   925	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   926	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   927	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   928	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   929	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   930	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   931	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   932	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   933	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   934	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
   935	                        pressedOption == null &&
   936	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   937	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   938	                    pressedMockField = null
   939	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   940	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   941	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   942	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   943	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   944	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   945	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   946	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   947	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   948	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   949	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   950	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   951	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   952	                    super.performClick()
   953	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
   954	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   955	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
   956	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   957	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   958	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
   959	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
   960	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   961	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   962	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
   963	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
   964	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   965	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
   966	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
   967	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   968	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   969	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   970	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   971	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   972	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
   973	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
   974	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
   975	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   976	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   977	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   978	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   979	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
   980	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
   981	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
   982	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   983	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
   984	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
   985	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
   986	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
   987	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
   988	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   989	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   990	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   991	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
   992	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
   993	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   994	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
   995	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
   996	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
   997	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   998	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   999	                        }
  1000	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1001	                    } else {
  1002	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1003	                    }
  1004	                    return true
  1005	                }
  1006	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1007	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1008	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1009	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1010	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1011	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
  1012	                val swipeDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1013	                val swipeProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1014	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  1015	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1016	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1017	                    swipeProgress >= PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_PROGRESS &&
  1018	                    swipeDirection != null
  1019	                val shouldNavigate = !shouldSwipeProfile && !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1020	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
  1021	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
  1022	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1023	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1024	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1025	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1026	                if (shouldSwipeProfile && swipeDirection != null) {
  1027	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1028	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1029	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1030	                    startPortraitProfileCarousel(swipeDirection, swipeProgress)
  1031	                    true
  1032	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1033	                    super.performClick()
  1034	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1035	                    true
  1036	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1037	                    super.performClick()
  1038	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1039	                    true
  1040	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1041	                    super.performClick()
  1042	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1043	                    true
  1044	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1045	                    performClick()
  1046	                    true
  1047	                } else {
  1048	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1049	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1050	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1051	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1052	                    false
  1053	                }
  1054	            }
  1055	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1056	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1057	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1058	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1059	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1060	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1061	                pressedMockField = null
  1062	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1063	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1064	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1065	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1066	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1067	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1068	                lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1069	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1070	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1071	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1072	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1073	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1074	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1075	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1076	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1077	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1078	                false
  1079	            }
  1080	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1081	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1082	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1083	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1084	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1085	        }
  1086	    }
  1087	
  1088	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1089	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1090	    }
  1091	
  1092	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1093	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1094	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1095	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1096	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1097	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1098	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1099	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1100	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1101	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1102	        }
  1103	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1104	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1105	        } else {
  1106	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1107	        }
  1108	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1109	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1110	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1111	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1112	    }
  1113	
  1114	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1115	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1116	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1117	        }
  1118	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1119	    }
  1120	
  1121	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1122	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1123	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1124	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1125	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1126	        }
  1127	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1128	    }
  1129	
  1130	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1131	        super.performClick()
  1132	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1133	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1134	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1135	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1136	        return true
  1137	    }
  1138	
  1139	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1140	        canvas: Canvas,
  1141	        stageLeft: Float,
  1142	        stageTop: Float,
  1143	        stageScale: Float,
  1144	        progress: Float,
  1145	        alpha: Float,
  1146	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1147	    ) {
  1148	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1149	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1150	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1151	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1152	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1153	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1154	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1155	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1156	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1157	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1158	        )
  1159	        val portraitCenteredLogo = Bounds(
  1160	            x = portraitLogo.x + portraitLogo.w / 2f -
  1161	                portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1162	            y = portraitStackCenter(0.5f) -
  1163	                portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1164	            w = portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE,
  1165	            h = portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE
  1166	        )
  1167	        val base = offsetBounds(
  1168	            lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitCenteredLogo, portraitAmount),
  1169	            focusShiftY
  1170	        )
  1171	        val final = offsetBounds(
  1172	            lerpBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1173	            focusShiftY
  1174	        )
  1175	
  1176	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1177	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1178	            return
  1179	        }
  1180	
  1181	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1182	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1183	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1184	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1185	    }
  1186	
  1187	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1188	        canvas: Canvas,
  1189	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1190	        yOffset: Float,
  1191	        alpha: Float
  1192	    ) {
  1193	        val slideDistance = portraitAvatarSize()
  1194	        val dragDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1195	        val dragProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1196	        val carouselProgress = portraitProfileCarouselProgress()
  1197	        val carouselDirection = portraitProfileCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1198	
  1199	        if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved && dragDirection != null && !isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1200	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1201	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1202	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - dragProgress), portraitProfileIndex,
  1203	                -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance
  1204	            )
  1205	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1206	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * dragProgress, portraitProfileIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1207	                direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance
  1208	            )
  1209	        } else if (isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1210	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1211	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - carouselProgress), portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex,
  1212	                -carouselDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance
  1213	            )
  1214	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1215	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * carouselProgress, portraitProfileCarouselToIndex,
  1216	                carouselDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance
  1217	            )
  1218	        } else {
  1219	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha, portraitProfileIndex, 0f)
  1220	        }
  1221	    }
  1222	
  1223	    private fun drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1224	        canvas: Canvas,
  1225	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1226	        yOffset: Float,
  1227	        alpha: Float,
  1228	        profileIndex: Int,
  1229	        offsetX: Float
  1230	    ) {
  1231	        val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[profileIndex].profile
  1232	        val avatarBitmap = when (profile) {
  1233	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveAvatar
  1234	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinAvatar
  1235	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyAvatar
  1236	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestAvatar
  1237	        }
  1238	        val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  1239	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  1240	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  1241	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  1242	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  1243	        }
  1244	        val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  1245	        val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  1246	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1247	            canvas,
  1248	            avatarBitmap,
  1249	            Bounds(
  1250	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f + offsetX,
  1251	                portraitAvatarTop() + yOffset,
  1252	                avatarSize,
  1253	                avatarSize
  1254	            ),
  1255	            stage.left,
  1256	            stage.top,
  1257	            stage.scale,
  1258	            alpha
  1259	        )
  1260	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1261	            canvas,
  1262	            nameBitmap,
  1263	            Bounds(
  1264	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f + offsetX,
  1265	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1266	                nameWidth,
  1267	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1268	            ),
  1269	            stage.left,
  1270	            stage.top,
  1271	            stage.scale,
  1272	            alpha
  1273	        )
  1274	    }
  1275	
  1276	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1277	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1278	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1279	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1280	            return
  1281	        }
  1282	
  1283	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1284	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1285	            0f,
  1286	            0f,
  1287	            0f,
  1288	            height.toFloat(),
  1289	            intArrayOf(
  1290	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1291	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1292	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1293	            ),
  1294	            null,
  1295	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1296	        )
  1297	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1298	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1299	
  1300	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1301	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1302	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1303	        }
  1304	    }
  1305	
  1306	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1307	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1308	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1309	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1310	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1311	    }
  1312	
  1313	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1314	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1315	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1316	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1317	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1318	    }
  1319	
  1320	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1321	        canvas: Canvas,
  1322	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1323	        bounds: Bounds,
  1324	        stageLeft: Float,
  1325	        stageTop: Float,
  1326	        stageScale: Float,
  1327	        alpha: Float
  1328	    ) {
  1329	        tempRect.set(
  1330	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1331	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1332	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1333	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1334	        )
  1335	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1336	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1337	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1338	    }

  1340	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1341	        canvas: Canvas,
  1342	        stageLeft: Float,
  1343	        stageTop: Float,
  1344	        stageScale: Float,
  1345	        yOffset: Float,
  1346	        alpha: Float
  1347	    ) {
  1348	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1349	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1350	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1351	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1352	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1353	            portraitAmount
  1354	        ) * stageScale
  1355	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1356	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1357	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1358	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1359	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1360	
  1361	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1362	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1363	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1364	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1365	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1366	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1367	            standardCenterY,
  1368	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1369	            portraitAmount
  1370	        )
  1371	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1372	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1373	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1374	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1375	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1376	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1377	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1378	        if (
  1379	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1380	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1381	        ) {
  1382	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1383	            canvas.drawText(
  1384	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1385	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1386	                createBaselineY,
  1387	                accountPromptPaint
  1388	            )
  1389	        }
  1390	        if (
  1391	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1392	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1393	        ) {
  1394	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1395	            canvas.drawText(
  1396	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1397	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1398	                signInBaselineY,
  1399	                formTitlePaint
  1400	            )
  1401	        }
  1402	        if (
  1403	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1404	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1405	        ) {
  1406	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1407	            canvas.drawText(
  1408	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1409	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1410	                forgotBaselineY,
  1411	                accountPromptPaint
  1412	            )
  1413	        }
  1414	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1415	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1416	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1417	    }

  1419	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1420	        canvas: Canvas,
  1421	        stageLeft: Float,
  1422	        stageTop: Float,
  1423	        stageScale: Float,
  1424	        progress: Float,
  1425	        formFocusMotion: Float,

  1427	    ) {
  1428	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1429	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1430	        }
  1431	
  1432	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)

  1434	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1435	            return
  1436	        }

  1438	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1439	            return
  1440	        }
  1441	
  1442	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1443	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1444	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()

  1447	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
  1448

  1450	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1451	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1452	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1453	
  1454	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1455	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1456	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1457	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1458	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1459	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1460	            canvas,
  1461	            fieldX,
  1462	            rememberMeY,
  1463	            stageLeft,
  1464	            stageTop,
  1465	            stageScale,
  1466	            alpha
  1467	        )
  1468	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1469	            canvas,
  1470	            stageLeft,
  1471	            stageTop,
  1472	            stageScale,
  1473	            alpha,
  1474	            yOffset,
  1475	            focusShiftY
  1476	        )
  1477	
  1478	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1479	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1480	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1481	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1482	    }

  1644	            alpha,
  1645	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TEXT_SIZE,
  1646	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_LINE_HEIGHT
  1647	        )
  1648	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1649	            canvas,
  1650	            stageLeft,
  1651	            stageTop,
  1652	            stageScale,
  1653	            alpha,
  1654	            yOffset,
  1655	            focusShiftY
  1656	        )
  1657	
  1658	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1659	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1660	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1661	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1662	    }
  1663	
  1664	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1665	        canvas: Canvas,
  1666	        stageLeft: Float,
  1667	        stageTop: Float,
  1668	        stageScale: Float,
  1669	        alpha: Float,
  1670	        yOffset: Float,
  1671	        focusShiftY: Float
  1672	    ) {
  1673	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1674	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1675	
  1676	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1677	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1678	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1679	
  1680	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1681	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1682	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1683	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1684	
  1685	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1686	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1687	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1688	                canvas,
  1689	                activeDropdown,
  1690	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1691	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1692	                stageLeft,
  1693	                stageTop,
  1694	                stageScale,
  1695	                alpha,
  1696	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1697	            )
  1698	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1699	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1700	            }
  1701	            return
  1702	        }
  1703	
  1704	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1705	        drawMockSignInField(
  1706	            canvas,
  1707	            activeField,
  1708	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1709	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1710	            stageLeft,
  1711	            stageTop,
  1712	            stageScale,
  1713	            alpha,
  1714	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1715	        )
  1716	    }

  1718	    private fun drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
  1719	        canvas: Canvas,
  1720	        flow: MockAccountFlow,
  1721	        stageLeft: Float,
  1722	        stageTop: Float,
  1723	        stageScale: Float,
  1724	        progress: Float,
  1725	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1726	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  1727	    ) {
  1728	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  1729	        activeMockFlow = flow
  1730	        try {
  1731	            drawMockSignInForm(
  1732	                canvas,
  1733	                stageLeft,
  1734	                stageTop,
  1735	                stageScale,
  1736	                progress,
  1737	                formFocusMotion,
  1738	                updateLabelAnimation
  1739	            )
  1740	        } finally {
  1741	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  1742	        }
  1743	    }

  1745	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1746	        canvas: Canvas,
  1747	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1748	        stageLeft: Float,
  1749	        stageTop: Float,
  1750	        stageScale: Float,
  1751	        alpha: Float
  1752	    ) {
  1753	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1754	        val sourceTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1755	        val targetTextSize = MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1756	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1757	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1758	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop +
  1759	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stageScale
  1760	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1761	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1762	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1763	        val targetBaselineY = stageTop + (fieldTopY - MOCK_FORM_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP) * stageScale
  1764	
  1765	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1766	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1767	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1768	        canvas.drawText(
  1769	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1770	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  1771	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1772	            formTitlePaint
  1773	        )
  1774	    }

  1838	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1839	        canvas: Canvas,
  1840	        x: Float,
  1841	        y: Float,
  1842	        stageLeft: Float,
  1843	        stageTop: Float,
  1844	        stageScale: Float,
  1845	        alpha: Float,
  1846	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1847	    ) {
  1848	        tempRect.set(
  1849	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1850	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1851	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  1852	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1853	        )
  1854	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  1855	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  1856	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  1857	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  1858	        val fillAlpha = when {
  1859	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  1860	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  1861	            pressed -> 92f
  1862	            enabled -> 68f
  1863	            else -> 18f
  1864	        }
  1865	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  1866	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  1867	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  1868	            pressed -> 238f
  1869	            enabled -> 210f
  1870	            else -> 118f
  1871	        }
  1872	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1873	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1874	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1875	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1876	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1877	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1878	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  1879	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  1880	        }
  1881	
  1882	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1883	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1884	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1885	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1886	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1887	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  1888	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  1889	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  1890	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  1891	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  1892	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  1893	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  1894	            formTitlePaint
  1895	        )
  1896	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  1897	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  1898	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  1899	            enabled -> 255f
  1900	            else -> 138f
  1901	        }).roundToInt()
  1902	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1903	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  1904	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  1905	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  1906	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  1907	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  1908	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  1909	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1910	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  1911	            canvas.drawLine(
  1912	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  1913	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  1914	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1915	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1916	                backChevronPaint
  1917	            )
  1918	            canvas.drawLine(
  1919	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1920	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1921	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  1922	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  1923	                backChevronPaint
  1924	            )
  1925	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  1926	        } else {
  1927	            tempRect.centerX()
  1928	        }
  1929	        canvas.drawText(
  1930	            buttonText,
  1931	            textX,
  1932	            baselineY,
  1933	            formTitlePaint
  1934	        )
  1935	    }
  1936	
  1937	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  1938	        canvas: Canvas,
  1939	        buttonRect: RectF,
  1940	        radius: Float,
  1941	        alpha: Float
  1942	    ) {
  1943	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  1944	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  1945	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  1946	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  1947	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  1948	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1949	            sweepLeft,
  1950	            buttonRect.top,
  1951	            sweepRight,
  1952	            buttonRect.top,
  1953	            intArrayOf(
  1954	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  1955	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  1956	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  1957	            ),
  1958	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  1959	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1960	        )
  1961	        canvas.save()
  1962	        tempPath.reset()
  1963	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  1964	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1965	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  1966	        canvas.restore()
  1967	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1968	    }

  1970	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoverySelector(
  1971	        canvas: Canvas,
  1972	        x: Float,
  1973	        y: Float,
  1974	        stageLeft: Float,
  1975	        stageTop: Float,
  1976	        stageScale: Float,

  1978	        formWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1979	    ) {
  1980	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1981	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1982	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1983	
  1984	        val labelWidth = formLabelPaint.measureText(MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_TEXT)
  1985	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale

  1987	            usernameTextWidth = formInputPaint.measureText(MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Username.displayText),
  1988	            passwordTextWidth = formInputPaint.measureText(MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password.displayText),

  1990	        )

  1992	            left = stageLeft + x * stageScale,

  1994	            labelWidth = labelWidth,
  1995	            boxWidth = optionBoxWidth
  1996	        )
  1997	        val rowTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  1998	        val rowCenterY = rowTop + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  1999	        val labelMetrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics

  2002	            layout.labelX,

  2005	        )

  2008	            canvas,

  2010	            layout.usernameBoxX,
  2011	            rowTop,
  2012	            layout.boxWidth,
  2013	            stageScale,
  2014	            alpha
  2015	        )
  2016	        drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2017	            canvas,
  2018	            MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password,
  2019	            layout.passwordBoxX,
  2020	            rowTop,
  2021	            layout.boxWidth,
  2022	            stageScale,
  2023	            alpha
  2024	        )
  2025	    }

  2027	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2028	        canvas: Canvas,
  2029	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  2030	        left: Float,
  2031	        top: Float,
  2032	        boxWidth: Float,
  2033	        stageScale: Float,
  2034	        alpha: Float
  2035	    ) {
  2036	        val right = left + boxWidth
  2037	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2038	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  2039	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2040	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2041	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2042	            pressed -> 82f
  2043	            selected -> 64f
  2044	            else -> 14f
  2045	        }).roundToInt()
  2046	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2047	            pressed -> 245f
  2048	            selected -> 235f
  2049	            else -> 142f
  2050	        }).roundToInt()
  2051	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2052	
  2053	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2054	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2055	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2056	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2057	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2058	
  2059	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2060	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  2061	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2062	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2063	        canvas.drawText(
  2064	            target.displayText,
  2065	            tempRect.centerX(),
  2066	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2067	            formInputPaint
  2068	        )
  2069	    }
