  2071	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  2072	        canvas: Canvas,
  2073	        text: String,
  2074	        centerX: Float,
  2075	        topY: Float,
  2076	        maxWidth: Float,
  2077	        stageLeft: Float,
  2078	        stageTop: Float,
  2079	        stageScale: Float,
  2080	        alpha: Float
  2081	    ): Float {
  2082	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2083	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2084	            canvas,
  2085	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2086	            centerX,
  2087	            topY,
  2088	            maxWidth,
  2089	            stageLeft,
  2090	            stageTop,
  2091	            stageScale,
  2092	            alpha,
  2093	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2094	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  2095	        )
  2096	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2097	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2098	            canvas,
  2099	            middleText,
  2100	            centerX,
  2101	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2102	            maxWidth,
  2103	            stageLeft,
  2104	            stageTop,
  2105	            stageScale,
  2106	            alpha,
  2107	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2108	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2109	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2110	        )
  2111	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2112	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2113	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2114	            canvas,
  2115	            bottomText,
  2116	            centerX,
  2117	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2118	            maxWidth,
  2119	            stageLeft,
  2120	            stageTop,
  2121	            stageScale,
  2122	            alpha,
  2123	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2124	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2125	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2126	        )
  2127	    }
  2128	
  2129	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2130	        canvas: Canvas,
  2131	        text: String,
  2132	        centerX: Float,
  2133	        topY: Float,
  2134	        maxWidth: Float,
  2135	        stageLeft: Float,
  2136	        stageTop: Float,
  2137	        stageScale: Float,
  2138	        alpha: Float,
  2139	        textSize: Float,
  2140	        lineHeight: Float,
  2141	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2142	    ): Float {
  2143	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2144	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2145	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2146	
  2147	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2148	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2149	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2150	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2151	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2152	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2153	        }
  2154	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2155	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2156	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2157	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2158	        }
  2159	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2160	    }
  2161	
  2162	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2163	        canvas: Canvas,
  2164	        columnX: Float,
  2165	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2166	        stageLeft: Float,
  2167	        stageTop: Float,
  2168	        stageScale: Float,
  2169	        alpha: Float
  2170	    ) {
  2171	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  2172	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  2173	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  2174	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  2175	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  2176	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  2177	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  2178	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2179	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2180	        canvas.drawText(
  2181	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  2182	            centerX,
  2183	            baselineY,
  2184	            formLabelPaint
  2185	        )
  2186	
  2187	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  2188	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  2189	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  2190	            avatarLeftX,
  2191	            avatarTopY,
  2192	            scaledAvatarSize,
  2193	            scaledAvatarSize
  2194	        )
  2195	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2196	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  2197	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  2198	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  2199	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  2200	
  2201	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  2202	
  2203	        canvas.save()
  2204	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2205	        if (
  2206	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  2207	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2208	            dragDirection != null &&
  2209	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2210	        ) {
  2211	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  2212	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2213	                canvas,
  2214	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2215	                avatarBounds,
  2216	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  2217	                stageLeft,
  2218	                stageTop,
  2219	                stageScale,
  2220	                alpha
  2221	            )
  2222	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2223	                canvas,
  2224	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  2225	                avatarBounds,
  2226	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  2227	                stageLeft,
  2228	                stageTop,
  2229	                stageScale,
  2230	                alpha
  2231	            )
  2232	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2233	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2234	                canvas,
  2235	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  2236	                avatarBounds,
  2237	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  2238	                stageLeft,
  2239	                stageTop,
  2240	                stageScale,
  2241	                alpha
  2242	            )
  2243	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2244	                canvas,
  2245	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  2246	                avatarBounds,
  2247	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  2248	                stageLeft,
  2249	                stageTop,
  2250	                stageScale,
  2251	                alpha
  2252	            )
  2253	        } else {
  2254	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2255	                canvas,
  2256	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2257	                avatarBounds,
  2258	                0f,
  2259	                stageLeft,
  2260	                stageTop,
  2261	                stageScale,
  2262	                alpha
  2263	            )
  2264	        }
  2265	        canvas.restore()
  2266	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2267	            canvas,
  2268	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2269	            avatarBounds,
  2270	            stageLeft,
  2271	            stageTop,
  2272	            stageScale,
  2273	            alpha
  2274	        )
  2275	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2276	            canvas,
  2277	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2278	            avatarBounds,
  2279	            stageLeft,
  2280	            stageTop,
  2281	            stageScale,
  2282	            alpha
  2283	        )
  2284	    }

  2326	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  2327	        canvas: Canvas,
  2328	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2329	        stageLeft: Float,
  2330	        stageTop: Float,
  2331	        stageScale: Float
  2332	    ) {
  2333	        tempPath.reset()
  2334	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2335	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2336	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2337	            mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds, stageScale),
  2338	            Path.Direction.CW
  2339	        )
  2340	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2341	    }

  2343	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  2344	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  2345	    }

  2347	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2348	        canvas: Canvas,
  2349	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2350	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2351	        stageLeft: Float,
  2352	        stageTop: Float,
  2353	        stageScale: Float,
  2354	        alpha: Float
  2355	    ) {
  2356	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2357	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2358	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2359	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2360	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2361	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2362	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2363	
  2364	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2365	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2366	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2367	        } else {
  2368	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2369	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2370	        }
  2371	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2372	    }

  2374	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2375	        canvas: Canvas,
  2376	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2377	        x: Float,
  2378	        y: Float,
  2379	        stageLeft: Float,
  2380	        stageTop: Float,
  2381	        stageScale: Float,
  2382	        alpha: Float,
  2383	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2384	    ) {
  2385	        tempRect.set(
  2386	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2387	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2388	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2389	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2390	        )
  2391	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2392	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2393	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2394	
  2395	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2396	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2397	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2398	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2399	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2400	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2401	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  2402	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  2403	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2404	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2405	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2406	        } else {
  2407	            0f
  2408	        }
  2409	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  2410	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2411	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2412	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2413	                canvas = canvas,
  2414	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2415	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  2416	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  2417	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2418	                alpha = textAlpha
  2419	            )
  2420	        }
  2421	        canvas.drawText(
  2422	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2423	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2424	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  2425	            formInputPaint
  2426	        )
  2427	
  2428	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  2429	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  2430	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  2431	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2432	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  2433	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2434	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2435	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2436	    }

  2438	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  2439	        canvas: Canvas,
  2440	        stageLeft: Float,
  2441	        stageTop: Float,
  2442	        stageScale: Float,
  2443	        alpha: Float
  2444	    ) {
  2445	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  2446	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  2447	
  2448	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2449	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  2450	            dropdown,
  2451	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  2452	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2453	        )
  2454	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2455	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2456	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  2457	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  2458	        val optionAlpha = 255
  2459	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2460	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2461	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  2462	        } else {
  2463	            0f
  2464	        }
  2465	
  2466	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  2467	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  2468	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  2469	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  2470	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  2471	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  2472	            } else {
  2473	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  2474	            }
  2475	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  2476	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  2477	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2478	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  2479	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  2480	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2481	
  2482	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2483	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  2484	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2485	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2486	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  2487	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2488	            val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2489	                (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2490	            } else {
  2491	                0f
  2492	            }
  2493	            if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2494	                drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2495	                    canvas = canvas,
  2496	                    bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2497	                    left = left + optionTextInset,
  2498	                    centerY = (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f,
  2499	                    iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2500	                    alpha = optionAlpha
  2501	                )
  2502	            }
  2503	            val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - iconLaneWidth - scrollbarReservedWidth)
  2504	                .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2505	            canvas.drawText(
  2506	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2507	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2508	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2509	                formInputPaint
  2510	            )
  2511	        }
  2512	
  2513	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2514	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2515	                canvas,
  2516	                dropdown,
  2517	                top,
  2518	                right,
  2519	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  2520	                stageScale,
  2521	                optionAlpha
  2522	            )
  2523	        }
  2524	    }
  2525	
  2526	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2527	        canvas: Canvas,
  2528	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  2529	        left: Float,
  2530	        centerY: Float,
  2531	        iconSize: Float,
  2532	        alpha: Int
  2533	    ) {
  2534	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  2535	        val width: Float
  2536	        val height: Float
  2537	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  2538	            width = iconSize
  2539	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  2540	        } else {
  2541	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  2542	            height = iconSize
  2543	        }
  2544	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  2545	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  2546	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  2547	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  2548	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  2549	    }
  2550	
  2551	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2552	        canvas: Canvas,
  2553	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2554	        top: Float,
  2555	        right: Float,
  2556	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  2557	        stageScale: Float,
  2558	        alpha: Int
  2559	    ) {
  2560	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2561	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2562	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2563	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  2564	
  2565	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  2566	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  2567	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2568	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2569	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2570	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  2571	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  2572	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  2573	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  2574	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2575	
  2576	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  2577	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2578	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2579	
  2580	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  2581	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2582	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2583	    }

  2585	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  2586	        canvas: Canvas,
  2587	        field: MockSignInField,
  2588	        x: Float,
  2589	        y: Float,
  2590	        stageLeft: Float,
  2591	        stageTop: Float,
  2592	        stageScale: Float,
  2593	        alpha: Float,
  2594	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2595	    ) {
  2596	        tempRect.set(
  2597	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2598	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2599	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  2600	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2601	        )
  2602	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2603	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2604	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2605	
  2606	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  2607	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  2608	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2609	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  2610	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  2611	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  2612	            labelProgress
  2613	        ) * stageScale
  2614	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2615	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2616	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2617	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2618	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2619	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  2620	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2621	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2622	            labelProgress
  2623	        )
  2624	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  2625	        canvas.drawText(
  2626	            label,
  2627	            labelX,
  2628	            labelBaselineY,
  2629	            formLabelPaint
  2630	        )
  2631	
  2632	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  2633	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  2634	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  2635	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  2636	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2637	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2638	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2639	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2640	
  2641	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  2642	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  2643	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2644	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  2645	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  2646	
  2647	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  2648	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  2649	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2650	                canvas.drawLine(
  2651	                    caretX,
  2652	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  2653	                    caretX,
  2654	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  2655	                    formCaretPaint
  2656	                )
  2657	            }
  2658	        }
  2659	    }
  2660	
  2661	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  2662	        canvas: Canvas,
  2663	        x: Float,
  2664	        y: Float,
  2665	        stageLeft: Float,
  2666	        stageTop: Float,
  2667	        stageScale: Float,
  2668	        alpha: Float
  2669	    ) {
  2670	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2671	        val boxLeft = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2672	        val boxTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2673	        val boxRight = boxLeft + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2674	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2675	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  2676	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  2677	
  2678	        rememberMeBoxFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2679	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2680	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.7f * stageScale
  2681	        tempRect.set(boxLeft, boxTop, boxRight, boxBottom)
  2682	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  2683	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  2684	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  2685	
  2686	        if (rememberMeChecked) {
  2687	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2688	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  2689	            canvas.drawLine(
  2690	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  2691	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,
  2692	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2693	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2694	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2695	            )
  2696	            canvas.drawLine(
  2697	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2698	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2699	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  2700	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  2701	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2702	            )
  2703	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  2704	        }
  2705	
  2706	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2707	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2708	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2709	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2710	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2711	        canvas.drawText(
  2712	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  2713	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  2714	            baselineY,
  2715	            rememberMeTextPaint
  2716	        )
  2717	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  2718	    }
  2719	
  2720	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2721	        canvas: Canvas,
  2722	        centerX: Float,
  2723	        topY: Float,
  2724	        stageLeft: Float,
  2725	        stageTop: Float,
  2726	        stageScale: Float,
  2727	        alpha: Float
  2728	    ) {
  2729	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  2730	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2731	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  2732	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2733	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2734	
  2735	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2736	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2737	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  2738	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  2739	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2740	    }

  2742	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2743	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2744	    }
  2745	
  2746	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2747	        source: Bitmap,
  2748	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2749	    ): Bitmap {
  2750	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2751	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2752	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2753	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2754	
  2755	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2756	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2757	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2758	        }
  2759	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2760	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2761	        }
  2762	    }
  2763	
  2764	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2765	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2766	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2767	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2768	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2769	
  2770	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2771	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2772	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2773	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2774	
  2775	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2776	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2777	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2778	            val dx = x - centerX
  2779	            val dy = y - centerY
  2780	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2781	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2782	            } else {
  2783	                pixels[index]
  2784	            }
  2785	        }
  2786	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2787	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2788	        }
  2789	    }
  2790	
  2791	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2792	        pixels: IntArray,
  2793	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2794	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2795	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2796	    ): BooleanArray {
  2797	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2798	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2799	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2800	        }
  2801	
  2802	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2803	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2804	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2805	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2806	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2807	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2808	
  2809	                mask[index] = true
  2810	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2811	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2812	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2813	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2814	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2815	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2816	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2817	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2818	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2819	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2820	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2821	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2822	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2823	                        )
  2824	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2825	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2826	                        }
  2827	                    }
  2828	                }
  2829	            }
  2830	        }
  2831	        return mask
  2832	    }
  2833	
  2834	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2835	        pixel: Int,
  2836	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2837	    ): Boolean {
  2838	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2839	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2840	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2841	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2842	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2843	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2844	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2845	
  2846	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2847	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2848	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2849	    }
  2850	
  2851	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2852	        return when {
  2853	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2854	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2855	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2856	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2857	            else -> null
  2858	        }
  2859	    }

  2862	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2863	    }

  2870	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2871	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2872	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2873	
  2874	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2875	
  2876	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2877	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  2878	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2879	            val halfWidth = (avatarSize / 2f + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING) * stage.scale
  2880	            val top = stage.top + portraitAvatarTop() * stage.scale
  2881	            val bottom = stage.top + (
  2882	                portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight() +
  2883	                    PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_BOTTOM_PADDING
  2884	                ) * stage.scale
  2885	            return if (x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) && y in top..bottom) {
  2886	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  2887	            } else {
  2888	                null
  2889	            }
  2890	        }
  2891	
  2892	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2893	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  2894	            val bounds = target.bounds
  2895	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  2896	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  2897	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  2898	            val dx = x - centerX
  2899	            val dy = y - centerY
  2900	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  2901	        }?.profile
  2902	    }

  3103	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3104	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  3105	
  3106	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3107	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  3108	        if (
  3109	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  3110	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  3111	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  3112	        ) {
  3113	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  3114	            lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = if (mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f) {
  3115	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  3116	            } else {
  3117	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  3118	            }
  3119	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3120	        }
  3121	
  3122	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  3123	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3124	        }
  3125	        return true
  3126	    }

  3139	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3140	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3141	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3142	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  3143	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3144	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  3145	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  3146	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  3147	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3148	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  3149	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  3150	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  3151	        }
  3152	
  3153	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  3154	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  3155	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  3156	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  3157	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  3158	        }
  3159	        return null
  3160	    }
  3161	
  3162	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdown? {
  3163	        if (
  3164	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  3165	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  3166	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  3167	        ) {
  3168	            return null
  3169	        }
  3170	
  3171	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  3172	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3173	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3174	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3175	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3176	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  3177	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3178	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  3179	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  3180	        val top = stage.top + yStart * stage.scale
  3181	        val bottom = top + mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown) * mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale
  3182	        return if (pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom) dropdown else null
  3183	    }
  3184	
  3185	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3186	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  3187	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  3188	
  3189	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3190	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3191	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  3192	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  3193	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  3194	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3195	        }
  3196	
  3197	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  3198	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  3199	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  3200	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3201	        }
  3202	        return true
  3203	    }

  3205	    private fun mockBackChevronHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  3206	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  3207	
  3208	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  3209	        if (activeMockFlow == null) return false
  3210	        return mockBackChevronContains(
  3211	            x,
  3212	            y,
  3213	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
  3214	            mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
  3215	            stage
  3216	        )
  3217	    }
  3218	
  3219	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3220	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  3221	
  3222	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  3223	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  3224	
  3225	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3226	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  3227	    }
  3228	
  3229	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  3230	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  3231	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  3232	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  3233	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  3234	    }

  3341	        columnX: Float,
  3342	        firstFieldY: Float,
  3343	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  3344	    ): Bounds {

  3346	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  3347	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  3348	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  3349	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  3350	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  3351	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  3352	        return Bounds(
  3353	            centerX - side / 2f,
  3354	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  3355	            side,
  3356	            side
  3357	        )
  3358	    }

  3391	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)

  3395	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field

  3397	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  3398	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3399	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3400	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  3401	            activeComposingText = ""
  3402	            focusedMockField = field

  3404	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()

  3409	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)

  3411	        }
  3412	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3413	    }

  3617	    private fun mockDropdownOpensUp(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  3618	        return dropdown != MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier
  3619	    }

  3622	        return if (mockDropdownOpensUp(dropdown)) MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_TEXT_SIZE else MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE
  3623	    }

  3649	    private fun mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  3650	        return dropdown != MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier
  3651	    }
  3652	
  3653	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3654	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  3655	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  3656	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT
  3657	        } else {
  3658	            optionCount
  3659	        }
  3660	        return min(optionCount, viewportCount)
  3661	    }
  3662	
  3663	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  3664	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3665	    }
  3666	
  3667	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3668	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3669	    }
  3670	
  3671	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3672	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3673	    }
  3674	
  3675	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  3676	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3677	    }
  3678	
  3679	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  3680	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3681	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3682	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  3683	    }
  3684	
  3685	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  3686	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3687	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  3688	            return
  3689	        }
  3690	
  3691	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  3692	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  3693	    }

  3779	            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()

  3830	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  3831	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  3832	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3833	        ) {
  3834	            1f
  3835	        } else {
  3836	            0f
  3837	        }
  3838	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3839	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3840	    }

  3844	    }

  3847	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return
  3848	        if (forgotPasswordSubmissionState != MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  3849	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3850	            return
  3851	        }
  3852	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS) return
  3853	
  3854	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3855	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3856	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3857	    }

  4018	    private fun updateMockFormLabelAnimation() {
  4019	        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4020	        val elapsed = (now - lastFormLabelAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
  4021	        lastFormLabelAnimationMillis = now
  4022	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()
  4023	        mockFieldLabelFloatProgress.keys.forEach { field ->
  4024	            mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] = moveToward(
  4025	                mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] ?: 0f,
  4026	                mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(field),
  4027	                step
  4028	            )
  4029	        }
  4030	    }

  4035	        lastFormFocusAnimationMillis = now
  4036	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_FORM_FOCUS_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat()
  4037	        mockFormFocusProgress = moveToward(mockFormFocusProgress, mockFormFocusTarget(), step)
  4038	    }

  4046	    }

  4048	    private fun updateMockInputDimAnimation() {
  4049	        val target = mockInputDimTarget()
  4050	        if (target > 0f) {
  4051	            mockInputDimActiveDropdown = expandedMockDropdown
  4052	            mockInputDimActiveField = if (expandedMockDropdown == null) focusedMockField else null
  4053	        }
  4054	
  4055	        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4056	        val elapsed = (now - lastMockInputDimAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
  4057	        lastMockInputDimAnimationMillis = now
  4058	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_INPUT_DIM_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat()
  4059	        mockInputDimProgress = moveToward(mockInputDimProgress, target, step)
  4060	
  4061	        if (mockInputDimProgress == 0f && target == 0f) {
  4062	            mockInputDimActiveField = null
  4063	            mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  4064	        }
  4065	    }

  4069	        return mockFieldLabelFloatProgress.any { (field, progress) ->
  4070	            progress != mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(field)
  4071	        }
  4072	    }

  4102	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  4103	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  4104	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  4105	        return if (
  4106	            focusedMockField != null ||
  4107	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  4108	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  4109	        ) {
  4110	            1f
  4111	        } else {
  4112	            0f
  4113	        }
  4114	    }
  4115	
  4116	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  4117	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  4118	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  4119	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  4120	    }

  4122	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  4123	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  4124	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  4125	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  4126	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  4127	        }
  4128	
  4129	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  4130	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  4131	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  4132	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  4133	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  4134	    }

  4227	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  4228	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText

  4245	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4246	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4247	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4248	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  4249	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4250	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4251	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4252	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  4253	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  4254	        activeComposingText = ""
  4255	        return true
  4256	    }
  4257	
  4258	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4259	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4260	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4261	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4262	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4263	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4264	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  4265	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  4266	        activeComposingText = composingText
  4267	        return true
  4268	    }
  4269	
  4270	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  4271	        activeComposingText = ""
  4272	        return true
  4273	    }
  4274	
  4275	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  4276	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4277	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4278	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  4279	            activeComposingText = ""
  4280	            return true
  4281	        }
  4282	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  4283	        activeComposingText = ""
  4284	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  4285	        return true
  4286	    }
  4287	
  4288	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4289	        activeComposingText = ""
  4290	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  4291	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  4292	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  4293	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  4294	        } else if (
  4295	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  4296	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  4297	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  4298	        ) {
  4299	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4300	        } else {
  4301	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4302	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  4303	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4304	        }
  4305	        return true
  4306	    }
  4307	
  4308	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4309	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4310	        return when (keyCode) {
  4311	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4312	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4313	            else -> {
  4314	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4315	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  4316	            }
  4317	        }
  4318	    }
  4319	
  4320	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  4321	        return buildString {
  4322	            input.forEach { character ->
  4323	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  4324	                    append(character)
  4325	                }
  4326	            }
  4327	        }
  4328	    }
  4329	
  4330	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  4331	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  4332	
  4333	        var startIndex = 0
  4334	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  4335	            startIndex += 1
  4336	        }
  4337	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  4338	    }
  4339	
  4340	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  4341	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  4342	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  4343	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  4344	                lines += ""
  4345	            }
  4346	
  4347	            var currentLine = ""
  4348	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  4349	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  4350	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  4351	                .forEach { word ->
  4352	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  4353	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  4354	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  4355	                    } else {
  4356	                        lines += currentLine
  4357	                        currentLine = word
  4358	                    }
  4359	                }
  4360	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  4361	                lines += currentLine
  4362	            }
  4363	        }
  4364	        return lines
  4365	    }
  4366	
  4367	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  4368	        return when {
  4369	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  4370	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  4371	            else -> current
  4372	        }
  4373	    }

  4375	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  4376	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  4377	
  4378	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  4379	        val portraitScale = portraitDesignScale()
  4380	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  4381	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  4382	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  4383	        return StageMetrics(
  4384	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  4385	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  4386	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  4387	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  4388	            scale = stageScale
  4389	        )
  4390	    }

  4399	    private fun portraitDesignScale(): Float {
  4400	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return 1f
  4401	
  4402	        val widthScale = width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
  4403	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return widthScale
  4404	
  4405	        val referenceDesignHeight = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * PORTRAIT_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_TO_WIDTH
  4406	        return min(widthScale, height / referenceDesignHeight)
  4407	    }

  4590	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  4591	        if (
  4592	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  4593	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  4594	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  4595	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  4596	        ) {
  4597	            return
  4598	        }
  4599	
  4600	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  4601	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4602	        activeComposingText = ""
  4603	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  4604	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4605	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4606	        pressedMockField = null
  4607	        pressedRememberMe = false
  4608	        pressedBackChevron = false
  4609	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4610	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  4611	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  4612	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f

  4718	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  4719	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  4720	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  4721	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4722	        return lerpFloat(
  4723	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  4724	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  4725	            progress
  4726	        )
  4727	    }
  4728	
  4729	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  4730	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  4731	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  4732	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4733	    }
  4734	
  4735	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  4736	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  4737	    }

  4742	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  4743	    }

  4748	    }

  4751	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  4752	    }

  4754	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  4755	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  4756	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  4757	            .roundToInt()
  4758	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  4759	            .toLong()
  4760	    }
  4761	
  4762	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  4763	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  4764	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  4765	
  4766	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  4767	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  4768	            activeMockFlow = null
  4769	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  4770	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  4771	            pressedMockField = null
  4772	            pressedRememberMe = false
  4773	            pressedBackChevron = false
  4774	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4775	            pressedMockDropdown = null

  4783	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()

  4786	        }
  4787	
  4788	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  4789	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  4790	    }

  4792	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  4793	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4794	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4795	        }

  4811	        }

  4813	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {

  4816	        }

  4819	            return ""
  4820	        }

  4823	            return ""
  4824	        }
  4825	
  4826	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4827	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  4828	        }
  4829	
  4830	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4831	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4832	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  4833	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4834	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4835	                else -> {
  4836	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4837	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  4838	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  4839	                    } else {
  4840	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  4841	                    }
  4842	                }
  4843	            }
  4844	        }
  4845	    }
  4846	}

  5001	
  5002	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  5003	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  5004	}
  5005	
  5006	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  5007	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  5008	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5009	}
  5010	
  5011	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  5012	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  5013	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5014	}
  5015	
  5016	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  5017	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  5018	}
  5019	
  5020	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  5021	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  5022	}
  5023	
  5024	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  5025	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5026	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  5027	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  5028	    return base + bounce
  5029	}
  5030	
  5031	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  5032	
  5033	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  5034	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  5035	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  5036	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  5037	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  5038	)
  5039	
  5040	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  5041	    x = bounds.x,
  5042	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  5043	    w = bounds.w,
  5044	    h = bounds.h
  5045	)
  5046	
  5047	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  5048	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5049	    return Color.argb(
  5050	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  5051	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  5052	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  5053	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  5054	    )
  5055	}
  5056	
  5057	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  5058	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  5059	}
  5060	
  5061	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  5062	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  5063	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  5064	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  5065	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  5066	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  5067	    return output
  5068	}
  5069	
  5070	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  5071	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  5072	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  5073	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  5074	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  5075	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  5076	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  5077	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  5078	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  5079	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  5080	    return output
  5081	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	### Standard tablet emulator
    32	
    33	Use the guarded launcher for the persistent low-load QA profile:
    34	
    35	```bash
    36	./start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
    37	```
    38	
    39	The launcher requests and verifies `1280x800` at `160 dpi`, two guest CPU cores,
    40	host GPU acceleration, and the repository-owned rounded tablet bezel before
    41	opening Cinerific. It refuses to continue if the running AVD does not match.
    42	Emulator logs are written to
    43	`~/Library/Logs/Cinerific/standard-emulator.log`.
    44	
    45	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    46	
    47	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    48	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    49	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.
